- Verified the bank “Always placeholder” preference lifecycle candidate. Active bank action toggles `Player.placeholders`, opening sends varbit 3755 from the current field, and `ItemContainer` reads that field on withdrawals. JSON and SQLite adapters both call `PlayerSave.fromPlayer()`. The copied Jagex 15 April 2016 update explicitly says the toggle persists over logout. Exact DB item keys 3053/3054 provide identity and placeholder links only, not persistence semantics. - Re-verified Public Hide behavior against the active revision-241 cache, current OSRS Wiki [Chat Interface](https://oldschool.runescape.wiki/w/Chat_Interface#Public), and runtime. Cache script 175 maps Public right-click options Show standard/Friends/none/Hide (ops 3/4/5/6) to public modes 0/1/2/3 through scripts 184/CHAT_SETFILTER; separate op 2 selects autochat mode 4. Chat-display script 90 treats both modes 2 and 3 as hidden from the chat panel, while the official rule distinguishes Show none (no public display) from Hide (panel hidden, overhead allowed). `PlayerRelations.setChatModes()` accepts modes 0–3, but `canReceivePublicChatFrom()` permits only mode 0 or friend senders in mode 1; both `ChatPacketListener.handleText()` message delivery and `PlayerSession.createActorUpdates()` forced-overhead serialization use that predicate. Thus mode 3 incorrectly suppresses overhead under Hide. Narrow candidate recorded in `02_Pending_Audits.md`; the previous line 470 is the earlier unresolved check. No matching Memory candidate was found; READ-ONLY-DB has no chat-mode contract.
